# Ported Ratwood content

Generated by `modular_redtard/tools/port_rockhill.py` from the Ratwood-2.0 code tree and the Rockhill map. Each type keeps its Ratwood path, so the map loads it unchanged; `parent_type` grafts the Ratwood branch onto a TG or module base. Re-run the tool after the map gains new types (the map generator fails on a missing turf or area and warns on a missing object).

Ratwood revision: `9139abc2da265927eda1f31d04364f942dcb6f4a` (2026-10-26).

## Items without a world sprite (44)

Ratwood names an `icon_state` its sheet does not have (a sprite chosen at runtime, or a broken one). The port writes `icon_state = null`, as TG does for an item with no sprite, so the TG icon tests pass and the item stays listed here.
